## 6,977. judge:sequence-mcq-existing-v1-00636

**Question type:** EC_number

<td valign="top"><strong>Question:</strong> Which pair of EC numbers best represents the catalytic activities associated with this protein?<br><br><strong>Choices:</strong><br><br><strong>A.</strong> 3.5.1.1 and 6.3.5.4<br><br><strong>B.</strong> 3.5.1.1 and 6.3.5.3<br><br><strong>C.</strong> 3.5.1.2 and 6.3.5.4<br><br><strong>D.</strong> 3.5.1.2 and 6.3.5.3<br><br><strong>Flash-Next response:</strong><br><br><br><br>1. reasoning: <br><br>To determine the EC numbers, I analyzed the protein sequence for characteristic domain motifs:<br><br>**Key sequence motifs identified:**<br>- &quot;CNGFQ&quot; motif: Contains a cysteine (C) that is characteristic of the catalytic triad (Cys-His-Asp) in a **glutamine amidotransferase (GATase) domain**, which hydrolyzes glutamine.<br>- &quot;IPIAHGFGRY&quot; motif: This is a hallmark motif found in **GMP synthase** (guanylate synthetase), specifically within the adenylation/synthase domain.<br>- &quot;PGGFSYGD&quot;: This motif is consistent with the GATase domain structure.<br><br>**Domain analysis:**<br>1. **GATase domain** → Hydrolyzes glutamine to glutamate + ammonia → This corresponds to **EC 3.5.1.2 (L-glutaminase)**, NOT EC 3.5.1.1 (asparaginase), since the enzyme uses glutamine as the nitrogen donor.<br><br>2. **Synthase domain** (with the IPIAHGF motif) → Catalyzes: XMP + ATP + Glutamine + H₂O → GMP + ADP + Pi + Glutamate → This corresponds to **EC 6.3.5.4 (GMP synthase)**.<br><br>**Eliminating options:**<br>- EC 3.5.1.1 (asparaginase) is incorrect because the enzyme uses glutamine, not asparagine → eliminates A and B<br>- EC 6.3.5.3 (CTP synthase) is incorrect based on the &quot;IPIAHGF&quot; motif which is specific to GMP synthase → eliminates D<br><br>This confirms the protein is **GMP synthase** with associated glutaminase activity.<br><br>2. answer: C</td>
<td valign="top"><strong>Question:</strong> A protein with the sequence below is being functionally classified.
